DESIGN.md

show
## Concept
The player guides a constantly moving dot through an endless tunnel, tapping to briefly slow time and thread tighter gaps.

## Player
The player controls a luminous dot that automatically advances forward through the tunnel. The only verb is "tap" (or click), which triggers a temporal slowdown.

## Core loop
The dot slides forward while obstacles scroll toward the player. The player watches the upcoming tunnel, decides when a gap is too narrow, and taps to slow the world, allowing the dot to slip through. After the slowdown ends, normal speed resumes and new obstacles appear, prompting the next decision.

## Hazards / Obstacles
- Moving walls that swing inward, creating narrow passages.
- Rotating shafts that spin faster than the dot’s speed, requiring precise timing.
- Pulsating barriers that expand and contract, opening only briefly.
- Static spikes that protrude from the tunnel floor or ceiling; they are harmless during slowdown but deadly at normal speed.

## Score
- Each obstacle successfully passed during a slowdown.
- Surviving a wave of obstacles without a slowdown.
- Collecting occasional glowing orbs that appear only during the slowdown.

## Win and lose
The player loses when the dot collides with any hazard while time is normal. Upon death, a “Game Over” overlay displays the final score and prompts a restart. The player wins by achieving a high score threshold or surviving a predefined number of waves, after which a celebratory “You Win!” overlay appears.

## Feel
A tense, rhythmic experience with a steady forward thrust interrupted by sharp, gratifying bursts of slowed time. A neon‑lit, futuristic tunnel aesthetic with a synth‑wave soundtrack enhances the sense of urgency and control.

## Why it's fun
The thrill of snapping time to the exact moment a narrow gap opens, letting the dot glide through just as the world rushes back to speed.

TASKS.md

show
1. HTML scaffold & meta.json — create index.html with header, canvas, restart button per CONVENTIONS.md and a matching meta.json file.
2. Game state initialization — declare player, hazards array, score, phase, constants (SAFE_RADIUS, slowdownDuration) at top of script.
3. Animation loop — implement startLoop() using requestAnimationFrame, cancel previous frame, try/catch, clear canvas each tick.
4. Input handling — add keys Set with keydown/keyup listeners, R key and restart button to call reset().
5. Player physics — update player position each frame using dt, enforce bounds, keep player always moving forward at constant speed.
6. Temporal slowdown — detect tap/click to trigger a brief reduction of global obstacle speed for slowdownDuration while player speed stays unchanged.
7. Hazard spawning — implement spawn timer, generate hazards outside SAFE_RADIUS, ensure initial safe period.
8. Hazard movement — update hazard positions each frame scaled by dt and current speed factor (affected by slowdown).
9. Collision detection — check player-hazard overlap, on hit set phase to "gameover" and stop gameplay.
10. Scoring system — increase score when player successfully passes a hazard during slowdown or after each hazard cleared, draw score on canvas.
11. Rendering — draw background, hazards, player (high contrast), HUD, and clear canvas each frame.
12. Game‑over overlay — dim screen, display "Game Over", final score, and restart instructions on canvas.
13. Observable contract — populate window.__game each frame with state, score, player.x/y/visible, and a short objective string.
14. Audio feedback — lazily create AudioContext, play beep on hit, score, and death events, wrapped in try/catch.

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Temporal Slowdown</title>
  <style>
    body { margin: 0; background: #0a0a0f; color: #e6e6f0; font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif; }
    .game-header { padding: 12px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid #1d1d2a; }
    .game-header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 18px; color: #7df9ff; }
    .game-header .game-description { margin: 4px 0; color: #a5a5b8; }
    .game-header .game-controls { margin: 4px 0; padding-left: 18px; color: #c8c8d8; }
    main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { background: #0f1320; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }
    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Temporal Slowdown</h1>
    <p class="game-description">The dot auto‑advances; tap to briefly slow the tunnel, letting you thread tighter gaps.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Space to activate slowdown</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
  // === CONFIG =============================================================
  const DOT_SPEED = 300; // px/sec constant speed
  const DOT_RADIUS = 8;
  const SAFE_RADIUS = 200; // pixels, safe area around player start
  const SLOWDOWN_DURATION = 1.5; // seconds, duration of slowdown effect
  const SLOWDOWN_FACTOR = 0.3; // slowdown factor for obstacles during slowdown
  const HAZARD_RADIUS = 8; // radius of hazard circles
  const HAZARD_SPEED = 250; // px/sec obstacle speed
  const MIN_SPAWN_INTERVAL = 1.0; // seconds
  const MAX_SPAWN_INTERVAL = 2.0; // seconds

  // === STATE ==============================================================
  const canvas = document.getElementById('game');
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  const restartBtn = document.getElementById('restart');

  let player; // { x, y }
  let hazards = []; // array of hazard objects
  let score = 0;
  let phase = 'playing'; // "playing" | "gameover"
  let keys; // Set of pressed keys
  let lastTime;
  let loopId = null;
  let audioCtx = null;
  let spawnTimer = 0; // seconds until next hazard spawn
  let dot; // player dot
  let slowdownTimer = 0; // seconds remaining for slowdown effect

  // === RESET ==============================================================
  function reset() {
    dot = { x: 0, y: canvas.height / 2 };
    keys = new Set();
    lastTime = performance.now();
    publishState();
    startLoop();
  }

  // === LOOP / UPDATE ======================================================
  function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      try {
        const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
        lastTime = now;
        // Clear canvas each tick
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
        update(dt);
        render();
        publishState();
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('loop error', e);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  function update(dt) {
    // Apply slowdown timer decrement
    if (slowdownTimer > 0) {
      slowdownTimer = Math.max(0, slowdownTimer - dt);
    }
    // Simple constant forward motion for dot
    dot.x += DOT_SPEED * dt;
    if (dot.x - DOT_RADIUS > canvas.width) dot.x = DOT_RADIUS;
    // Hazard movement
    const speedFactor = slowdownTimer > 0 ? SLOWDOWN_FACTOR : 1;
    for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const h = hazards[i];
      h.x -= HAZARD_SPEED * speedFactor * dt;
      // Remove off-screen hazards
      if (h.x + HAZARD_RADIUS < 0) {
        hazards.splice(i, 1);
      }
    }
  }

  // === RENDER =============================================================
  function render() {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#0f1320';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ff52ae';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(dot.x, dot.y, DOT_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }

  // === INPUT ==============================================================
  document.addEventListener('keydown', ev => {
    if (ev.key === 'r' || ev.key === 'R') { reset(); return; }
    keys.add(ev.key);
    // placeholder: space triggers beep (slowdown effect placeholder)
    if (ev.key === ' ') beep(440, 0.1);
  });
  document.addEventListener('keyup', ev => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
  restartBtn.addEventListener('click', reset);

  // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
  function beep(freq, durSec) {
    try {
      if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
      osc.type = 'square';
      osc.frequency.value = freq;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      osc.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
      osc.start();
      osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    } catch {}
  }

  // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
  function publishState() {
    window.__game = {
      state: 'playing',
      score: 0,
      player: {
        x: Math.round(dot.x),
        y: Math.round(dot.y),
        visible: dot.x + DOT_RADIUS >= 0 && dot.x - DOT_RADIUS <= canvas.width && dot.y + DOT_RADIUS >= 0 && dot.y - DOT_RADIUS <= canvas.height,
      },
      objective: 'Tap to briefly slowdown the tunnel and thread tight gaps.',
    };
  }

  // === BOOT ===============================================================
  reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

Never make the player wait.\n92: 2. **Clear feedback** — the player always knows when something happened. On restart you MUST `cancelAnimationFrame` any pending loop before starting a new one (otherwise loops stack on each restart and the game eventually slows to a crawl).\n95: 5. **Progressive difficulty** — start gentle so the player can learn, then ramp up. A game that violates any of these is broken even if it runs without errors. After your implementation finishes, the validator loads the page in a real browser and runs an 8-second playtest with synthetic input — failures here are reported back to you and must be fixed before the game ships.\n103: \n104: ### 1. The player can lose\n105: \n106: There must be a collision check (or other end condition) that fires `gameOver` and stops the active gameplay loop. \"Game runs forever, no death\" is broken. Examples that have failed in the past: collision check compared a value against itself; \"spike\" collision only clamped position instead of killing the player; obstacles never reached the player.\n107: \n108: ### 2. Now hazard movement loop:

```js
for (let i = hazards.length -1; i>=0; i--) {
    const h = hazards[i];
    h.x -= HAZARD_SPEED * speedFactor * dt;
    // collision
    const dx = dot.x - h.x;
    const dy = dot.y - h.y;
    if (dx*dx + dy*dy < (DOT_RADIUS + HAZARD_RADIUS)*(DOT_RADIUS + HAZARD_RADIUS)) {
        phase = 'gameover';
        beep(110, 0.25);
        // maybe break
    }
    // off-screen left
    if (h.x + HAZARD_RADIUS < 0) {
        score++;
        beep(880, 0.05);
        hazards.splice(i, 1);
        continue;
    }
}
```

Implementation:

```
function render() {
  // background
  ctx.fillStyle = '#0f1320';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  // hazards
  ctx.fillStyle = '#7df9ff';
  for (const h of hazards) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(h.x, h.y, HAZARD_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }
  // player dot
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ff52ae';
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(dot.x, dot.y, DOT_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
  // HUD
  ctx.fillStyle = '#e6e6f0';
  ctx.font = '16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
  // game over overlay
  if (phase === 'gameover') {
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10,10,15,0.7)';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.font = '44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ff52ae';
    ctx.fillText('Game Over', canvas.width/2, canvas.height/2 - 20);
    ctx.font = '20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
    ctx.fillStyle = '#e6e6f0';
    ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, canvas.width/2, canvas.height/2 + 14);
    ctx.font = '16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
    ctx.fillStyle = '#a5a5b8';
    ctx.fillText('Press R or click Restart', canvas.width/2, canvas.height/2 + 42);
  }
}
```

Now observable contract: modify publishState to set state based on phase. Also update score.

```
function publishState() {
  window.__game = {
    state: phase === 'playing' ? 'playing' : 'gameover',
    score,
    player: {
      x: Math.round(dot.x),
      y: Math.round(dot.y),
      visible: dot.x >= 0 && dot.x <= canvas.width && dot.y >= 0 && dot.y <= canvas.height,
    },
    objective: 'Tap to briefly slowdown the tunnel and thread tight gaps.'
  };
}
```

prompt (13,887 chars)
## Reference example: "breakout"

The following is a complete, validator-passing game. **Use it as a structural template** — your output must follow the same section ordering, the same patterns for the loop / restart / observable contract, and the same overall code style. Do NOT reproduce this game; you are building a different game described in the prompt below. The example exists only to anchor structure.

Notice in particular:
- The `// === SECTION ===` divider banners and their fixed order: CONFIG → STATE → RESET → LOOP/UPDATE → RENDER → INPUT → AUDIO → OBSERVABLE CONTRACT → BOOT.
- Plain object literals for game state (no `class`).
- A `keys` Set polled each frame instead of mutating game state from key handlers.
- `if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` at the top of `startLoop()`.
- The animation loop body wrapped in `try/catch`.
- `publishState()` called once per frame at the end of the tick.
- `window.__game` populated honestly (player.visible reflects actual viewport position).
- AudioContext initialized lazily inside `beep()`, wrapped in try/catch.

### Example `meta.json`
```json
{
  "title": "Neon Breaker",
  "description": "Bounce the ball with the paddle and clear every brick. One ball, no second chances.",
  "controls": [
    "Arrow keys or A/D to move the paddle",
    "Hold Space to launch faster",
    "Press R or click Restart"
  ],
  "tags": ["arcade", "breakout", "bouncing"]
}
```

### Example `index.html`
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Neon Breaker</title>
  <style>
    body { margin: 0; background: #0a0a0f; color: #e6e6f0; font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif; }
    .game-header { padding: 12px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid #1d1d2a; }
    .game-header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 18px; color: #7df9ff; }
    .game-header .game-description { margin: 4px 0; color: #a5a5b8; }
    .game-header .game-controls { margin: 4px 0; padding-left: 18px; color: #c8c8d8; }
    main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { background: #0f1320; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }
    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Neon Breaker</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Bounce the ball with the paddle and clear every brick. One ball, no second chances.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Arrow keys or A/D to move the paddle</li>
      <li>Hold Space to launch faster</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
  // === CONFIG =============================================================
  const PADDLE_W = 110;
  const PADDLE_H = 14;
  const PADDLE_Y = 560;
  const PADDLE_SPEED = 700;       // px/sec

  const BALL_R = 7;
  const BALL_BASE_SPEED = 360;    // px/sec at start
  const BALL_MAX_SPEED  = 720;
  const BALL_SPEEDUP_PER_HIT = 6; // px/sec added per brick

  const BRICK_ROWS = 6;
  const BRICK_COLS = 12;
  const BRICK_W = 60;
  const BRICK_H = 20;
  const BRICK_GAP = 4;
  const BRICK_TOP = 60;
  const BRICK_LEFT = (800 - (BRICK_COLS * (BRICK_W + BRICK_GAP) - BRICK_GAP)) / 2;
  const ROW_COLORS = ["#ff52ae", "#ff8c52", "#ffd23f", "#a3ff52", "#52ffae", "#7df9ff"];

  // === STATE ==============================================================
  const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

  let paddle;        // { x } center x
  let ball;          // { x, y, vx, vy, speed }
  let bricks;        // array of { x, y, w, h, color, alive }
  let score;
  let bricksLeft;
  let phase;         // "playing" | "won" | "lost"
  let keys;          // pressed key set
  let highScore = 0;
  let lastTime;
  let loopId = null;
  let audioCtx = null;

  function reset() {
    paddle = { x: 400 };
    ball = { x: 400, y: 480, vx: 0, vy: 0, speed: BALL_BASE_SPEED };
    launchBall();
    bricks = [];
    for (let r = 0; r < BRICK_ROWS; r++) {
      for (let c = 0; c < BRICK_COLS; c++) {
        bricks.push({
          x: BRICK_LEFT + c * (BRICK_W + BRICK_GAP),
          y: BRICK_TOP + r * (BRICK_H + BRICK_GAP),
          w: BRICK_W, h: BRICK_H,
          color: ROW_COLORS[r % ROW_COLORS.length],
          alive: true,
        });
      }
    }
    bricksLeft = bricks.length;
    score = 0;
    phase = "playing";
    keys = new Set();
    lastTime = performance.now();
    publishState();
    startLoop();
  }

  function launchBall() {
    // Always launch upward, mild horizontal lean either side.
    const angle = (-Math.PI / 2) + (Math.random() * 0.6 - 0.3);
    ball.vx = Math.cos(angle) * ball.speed;
    ball.vy = Math.sin(angle) * ball.speed;
  }

  function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      try {
        const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
        lastTime = now;
        update(dt);
        render();
        publishState();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("loop error", err);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  // === UPDATE =============================================================
  function update(dt) {
    if (phase !== "playing") return;

    // Paddle.
    const dirX = (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D") ? 1 : 0)
               - (keys.has("ArrowLeft")  || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A") ? 1 : 0);
    const speedMul = keys.has(" ") ? 1.6 : 1;
    paddle.x += dirX * PADDLE_SPEED * speedMul * dt;
    paddle.x = clamp(paddle.x, PADDLE_W / 2, canvas.width - PADDLE_W / 2);

    // Ball motion.
    ball.x += ball.vx * dt;
    ball.y += ball.vy * dt;

    // Walls.
    if (ball.x < BALL_R) { ball.x = BALL_R; ball.vx = Math.abs(ball.vx); beep(220, 0.04); }
    if (ball.x > canvas.width - BALL_R) { ball.x = canvas.width - BALL_R; ball.vx = -Math.abs(ball.vx); beep(220, 0.04); }
    if (ball.y < BALL_R) { ball.y = BALL_R; ball.vy = Math.abs(ball.vy); beep(220, 0.04); }

    // Floor — lose.
    if (ball.y > canvas.height + BALL_R) {
      phase = "lost";
      if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
      beep(110, 0.25);
      return;
    }

    // Paddle.
    const px = paddle.x, py = PADDLE_Y;
    if (ball.vy > 0
        && ball.y + BALL_R >= py
        && ball.y - BALL_R <= py + PADDLE_H
        && ball.x >= px - PADDLE_W / 2 - BALL_R
        && ball.x <= px + PADDLE_W / 2 + BALL_R) {
      // Bounce direction depends on where it hit on the paddle.
      const offset = (ball.x - px) / (PADDLE_W / 2); // -1..1
      const angle = (-Math.PI / 2) + offset * 1.0;   // up-ish, leaning side
      ball.vx = Math.cos(angle) * ball.speed;
      ball.vy = Math.sin(angle) * ball.speed;
      ball.y = py - BALL_R - 0.1;
      beep(540, 0.05);
    }

    // Bricks.
    for (const b of bricks) {
      if (!b.alive) continue;
      if (ball.x + BALL_R < b.x || ball.x - BALL_R > b.x + b.w) continue;
      if (ball.y + BALL_R < b.y || ball.y - BALL_R > b.y + b.h) continue;

      b.alive = false;
      bricksLeft--;
      score += 10;
      ball.speed = Math.min(BALL_MAX_SPEED, ball.speed + BALL_SPEEDUP_PER_HIT);

      // Decide which side was hit by comparing penetrations.
      const overlapX = Math.min(ball.x + BALL_R - b.x, b.x + b.w - (ball.x - BALL_R));
      const overlapY = Math.min(ball.y + BALL_R - b.y, b.y + b.h - (ball.y - BALL_R));
      if (overlapX < overlapY) ball.vx = -ball.vx; else ball.vy = -ball.vy;

      // Renormalize to current speed.
      const m = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
      ball.vx = (ball.vx / m) * ball.speed;
      ball.vy = (ball.vy / m) * ball.speed;
      beep(880, 0.05);
      break;
    }

    if (bricksLeft === 0) {
      phase = "won";
      if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
      beep(990, 0.2);
    }
  }

  // === RENDER =============================================================
  function render() {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#0f1320";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

    // Bricks.
    for (const b of bricks) {
      if (!b.alive) continue;
      ctx.fillStyle = b.color;
      ctx.fillRect(b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h);
    }

    // Paddle.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
    ctx.fillRect(paddle.x - PADDLE_W / 2, PADDLE_Y, PADDLE_W, PADDLE_H);

    // Ball.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, BALL_R, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();

    // HUD.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
    ctx.textAlign = "right";
    ctx.fillText(`Bricks ${bricksLeft}  ·  Best ${highScore}`, canvas.width - 12, 22);

    if (phase === "lost" || phase === "won") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(10,10,15,0.7)";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillStyle = phase === "won" ? "#52ffae" : "#ff52ae";
      ctx.fillText(phase === "won" ? "You Win!" : "Game Over", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 20);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 14);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 42);
    }
  }

  // === INPUT ==============================================================
  document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
    if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
    keys.add(ev.key);
  });
  document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
  restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

  // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
  function beep(freq, durSec) {
    try {
      if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
      osc.type = "square";
      osc.frequency.value = freq;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
      osc.start(); osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    } catch {}
  }

  // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
  function publishState() {
    window.__game = {
      state: phase === "playing" ? "playing" : "gameover",
      score,
      player: {
        x: paddle.x,
        y: PADDLE_Y + PADDLE_H / 2,
        visible: paddle.x >= 0 && paddle.x <= canvas.width,
      },
      objective: "Bounce the ball with the paddle to break every brick.",
    };
  }

  // === HELPERS ============================================================
  function clamp(v, lo, hi) { return v < lo ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v); }

  // === BOOT ===============================================================
  reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

The startLoop currently defined:

```
function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      try {
        const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
        lastTime = now;
        update(dt);
        render();
        publishState();
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('loop error', e);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
```

```
function update(dt) {
  // Player movement
  dot.x += DOT_SPEED * dt;
  if (dot.x - DOT_RADIUS > canvas.width) dot.x = -DOT_RADIUS;

  // Hazard movement
  for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const hz = hazards[i];
    hz.x -= HAZARD_SPEED * dt;
    if (hz.x + HAZARD_RADIUS < 0) {
      hazards.splice(i, 1);
    }
  }

  // Spawn timer
  spawnTimer -= dt;
  if (spawnTimer <= 0) {
    spawnHazard();
    // reset timer to random interval
    spawnTimer = Math.random() * (MAX_SPAWN_INTERVAL - MIN_SPAWN_INTERVAL) + MIN_SPAWN_INTERVAL;
  }
}
```

Implementation:

function update(dt) {
    // Apply slowdown timer decrement
    if (slowdownTimer > 0) {
        slowdownTimer = Math.max(0, slowdownTimer - dt);
    }
    // Player dot movement (forward)
    dot.x += DOT_SPEED * dt;
    if (dot.x - DOT_RADIUS > canvas.width) dot.x = -DOT_RADIUS;
    // Hazards movement
    const speedFactor = slowdownTimer > 0 ? SLOWDOWN_FACTOR : 1;
    for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const h = hazards[i];
        h.x -= HAZARD_SPEED * speedFactor * dt;
        // Remove off-screen hazards
        if (h.x + HAZARD_RADIUS < 0) {
            hazards.splice(i, 1);
        }
    }
}

Your job in this stage is to break the implementation into a numbered list of small, ordered tasks. The next stage will work through this list one task at a time, in a single LLM session, reloading the page after each task and capturing console errors for you to fix.

Title: Temporal Slowdown
Concept: The dot auto‑advances at constant speed; tapping activates a brief slowdown of the entire tunnel, reducing obstacle velocity while the dot keeps moving, letting you thread tighter gaps before normal flow resumes.

**You must produce one output file: `TASKS.md` in the working directory.** Use your write/edit tool to create this file on disk. After your turn ends, the validator runs `readFile("TASKS.md")` — if the file does not exist on disk, validation fails and your work is wasted. **Do NOT reply with the task list as inline chat text.** The list must be written to the file.

**Tool calls MUST be emitted as actual function/tool invocations — NOT described or simulated inside your reasoning/analysis text.** Writing tool-shaped JSON like `{"filePath":"TASKS.md","content":"..."}` *inside your chain-of-thought* does NOT invoke any tool. It is just text in the analysis channel that nothing reads or executes. The same goes for `{"pattern":"**/*"}` etc. — those only do something when emitted as a real `tool_use` event from the action channel, never when written into your thinking. Several prior runs have failed by hallucinating tool calls inside reasoning; do not repeat that pattern. If at any point you "describe" a tool call in your thinking instead of invoking it, you must immediately invoke the real tool afterward. The format MUST be a numbered list, one task per line, each line starting with `<number>.`:

```
1. <Task title> — <one-sentence description of what to do>
2. <Task title> — <one-sentence description>
...
```

The next stage parses tasks by matching `^\d+\.` at the start of each line. Don't use bullet sub-lists, don't number sub-tasks (1.1, 1.2). One number, one task, one line.

### What makes a good task

- **Small enough** to complete in a single coding turn (~50–150 lines of edits).
- **Testable**: after this task, what does the page do that it didn't before?
- **Leaves the file runnable**: at the end of the task, no syntax errors, no uncaught exceptions on load. Even if the game isn't playable yet, it must load.
- **Specific**: don't say "implement player". Say "implement player movement: arrow keys set keys.left/right; in update(), apply acceleration in the pressed direction with cap at MAX_SPEED, friction otherwise; clamp x to canvas bounds."
- **Concrete numbers**: this is where pixel sizes, ms intervals, and px/sec speeds live. Pick them. The implement stage will use them verbatim.

### Required coverage (in roughly this order)

Cover at minimum, ordered as below. Add tasks if the design demands more (powerups, level progression, screen wrap, etc):

1. **HTML scaffold + meta.json**: header (h1, description, controls list), canvas, restart button per CONVENTIONS.md. Stub `<script>` block (empty). meta.json matching header text.
2. **Game state objects**: player, hazards, score, phase using plain object literals at the top of the script. Set initial values.
3. **requestAnimationFrame loop**: startLoop() with `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` discipline at the top. Body wrapped in try/catch. Calls update(dt) and render(). Don't draw anything yet besides clearing the canvas.
4. **Input**: a `keys` Set populated by document keydown/keyup. R-key restart hook. Restart button click hook. Both call the same reset() function.
5. **Player movement**: dt-based physics tied to the key inputs from task 4. Verify each control listed in meta.json actually changes a value.
6. **Hazards**: data structure, spawn rules with a SAFE_RADIUS so nothing spawns on top of the player at t=0, motion (dt-scaled).
7. **Collision detection**: player vs hazards. On hit, transition phase to "gameover" and stop the active gameplay.
8. **Score**: discrete events that increase score, drawn on canvas at top-left.
9. **Render**: clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player, HUD. Player must be the highest-contrast object on screen.
10. **Game-over overlay**: dim background, "Game Over", final score, "Press R or click Restart".
11. **Observable contract**: populate `window.__game = { state, score, player:{x,y,visible}, objective }` every frame. `state` is "playing" | "gameover". `objective` is a 10–200 char single-sentence description.
12. **Audio**: lazy-init AudioContext inside a beep() helper, wrapped in try/catch. Hook into hit / score / death events.

Potential tasks:

1. **Setup HTML scaffold & meta.json** — create index.html with required header, canvas, restart button, and meta.json matching the game's title, description, and controls.
2. **Initialize game state objects** — Define player, hazards array, score, phase, and constants (e.g., SAFE_RADIUS) inside a script tag.
3. **Implement animation loop** — Add startLoop() with requestAnimationFrame, cancel previous loop, try/catch, clear canvas each frame.
4. **Setup input handling** — Add keys Set with keydown/keyup listeners, R key restart, restart button click, both calling reset().
5. **Implement player movement** — Update player position each frame based on keys, dt physics, and keep player within canvas bounds.
6. **Create hazard spawning system** — Define hazard objects, spawn timer, ensure they spawn outside SAFE_RADIUS and with safe initial timing.
7. **Add hazard movement** — Update hazard positions each frame using dt, move toward player (or leftwards), remove off-screen hazards.
8. **Implement collision detection** — Detect player-hazard overlap, set game phase to "gameover", stop gameplay.
9. **Add scoring logic** — Increment score when player successfully passes a hazard during slowdown (or on passing obstacles), display score on canvas.
10. **Render game elements** — Draw background, hazards, player (high-contrast), and HUD (score) each frame.
11. **Implement temporal slowdown mechanic** — Detect tap/click, reduce global obstacle speed for a short duration while player speed unchanged.
12. **Game-over overlay** — Dim background, display "Game Over", final score, restart instructions on canvas.
13. **Expose observable contract** — Populate window.__game each frame with state, score, player.x/y/visible, and objective string.
14. **Add audio system** — Lazy-create AudioContext, play beep on hit, score, death events, with try/catch.
15. **Finalize cleanup & polish** — Ensure restart resets all variables, cancels prior loop, and re-initializes game; verify no console errors.
